## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you find yourself still, as the room remains unyielding to your presence. The bed, cup, book, and door stand as silent sentinels in the cramped space. A faint sensation of uncleanliness permeates your skin, a stark reminder of the environment. Despite your attempt to speak with more conviction, the words seem to slip from your grasp, dissolving in the quiet. The cell remains unperturbed, waiting with an eerie tranquility.

## Turn 2
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you find yourself alone, the quiet echoing off the stone walls. The bed, cup, book, and door are the only companions in this small, unassuming space. Your skin feels unclean, a constant reminder of your surroundings.

You wait, your breaths matching the silence of the room. Time seems to stretch on, the words you speak coming out thinner than you intend. The cell keeps its quiet, a testament to the routines and rules that govern this place. Despite your efforts, nothing changes. You wait, and the cell waits with you.

## Turn 3
> wait

The observation slit in the door creaks open, revealing a figure beyond. Their words are a mix of muffled noises and deliberate gestures, the meaning clear despite the lack of clarity: they want you to step back from the door. You remain stationary, the cell's silence your only response. The figure's gaze lingers, before the slit closes with a soft click. The cell remains quiet, the air heavy with unspoken tension. The figure's demand echoes in your mind, a reminder of your confined existence. The cold stone walls seem to close in, a stark reminder of your isolation. Your heart pounds in your chest, the rhythm a constant reminder of your existence. The cell, as always, keeps its quiet.

## Turn 4
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you wait, the air heavy with tension. The large, silent staff member beyond the door continues to gesture, their repeated words a mere echo of the command: step away from the door. The cell remains quiet, save for the faint sound of your own breath. Your skin feels unclean, a constant reminder of the confined space you inhabit, and the words that leave your mouth seem thinner than you intend. The staff member's gaze never wavers, their patience unbroken. The cell keeps its quiet, the tension unresolved.

## Turn 7
> I will not move

In the dimly lit corridor, the same bolts as the cell door echo your recent departure. Hands, rough and steadfast, remain clamped on your arms, guiding you forward. The institution's pace is unyielding, a testament to its unwavering control. But this time, you resist. You stand firm, your feet planted steadfastly against the cold, unyielding floor. The hands on your arms pause, a momentary disruption in the institution's rhythm. The air around you seems to thicken, a palpable tension born from the contrast between your will and theirs. Yet, the corridor remains unmoving, the hands on your arms unyielding, and the institution moves forward, unperturbed by your defiance.

## Turn 8
> stand in front of the door

In the dimly lit corridor, you find yourself standing before a massive door, its surface rough and unyielding. The door, devoid of an inner handle, stands as a stark reminder of your confinement. A slit, narrow and obscured, offers a glimpse of the world beyond. Your hands, bound, restrict your movement, and the air around you carries an unsettling chill. The institution's pace remains unwavering, the staff members, one large and silent, the other young and tense, continue to guide you forward, their intentions clear. The door, your momentary obstacle, has not budged despite your presence.

## Turn 10
> struggle

In the sterile washroom, under the watchful eyes of two staff members, one large and silent, the other tense and rule-bound, the basin remains, unyielding. The water remains, waiting. The soap, medicinal and cold, remains nearby. Your struggle, though earnest, goes unheeded, as they continue to wait, their patience worn thin but unwavering. The air remains heavy with the scent of soap and a hint of unease. Your skin, despite your struggle, remains unclean, a stark reminder of the routine procedure that looms ahead. The words you utter, though meant to defy, come out thin and weak, a testament to the power dynamic at play. The tension, unresolved, lingers like a heavy fog, pervading the room. The basin, the water, the soap - they remain, a stark contrast to your planted feet and unyielding hands.

The large, silent staff member continues to stand by the basin, their gaze steady and unyielding. The younger, tense one remains at attention, their fingers tapping against the washroom's cool stone wall. The air between them is thick with anticipation, the scent of medicinal soap lingering.

Despite your attempt to express resistance, your words come out thinner than you intend, lost in the quiet of the room. The water in the basin does not recede, and the younger staff member does not look away. Your hands, bound or not, remain still, a stark contrast to the fluidity of the water that waits.

The basin, the soap, the large and young staff members - they are all reminders of what each wants: to wash you. ## Turn 12
> refuse to wash

In the mess, amidst the hum of the facility, you refuse the food before you, your actions met with quiet disapproval from the staff. The large, silent one watches you, unyielding, while the younger, tense one maintains a rigid demeanor. Ruan, their eyes lucid yet fractured, seems to understand the defiance in your gesture.

The spoon and bowl, though still present, feel out of reach, as if they're symbols of a different world. The staff continue their grip, unwavering, as water and soap arrive, regardless of your intent. A sense of unease settles beneath your ribs, a reminder of the rules that bind you here. The bowl remains untouched, a testament to your refusal, as your stomach voices its discontent subtly. The meal continues, the staff waiting for you to eat or at least leave it alone.

## Turn 13
> refuse the food

In the dimly lit mess, the air heavy with the scent of food, you find yourself at a table, surrounded by staff members who expect you to eat. One, larger and more silent, continues to grip you firmly, while another, younger and more tense, watches attentively. Ruan, lucid and fractured, watches too. The table holds a warm bowl of food, a spoon, and water and soap at the ready.

Refusing their expectations, you vocalize your refusal, but the words come out thinner than you intended. ## Turn 14
> push the bowl away

In the dimly lit mess, the bowl, once before you, now lies shattered on the floor. The remnants of your meal spill out, creating a mess that echoes the discord in the air. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, watch you with eyes that hold a mix of disappointment and determination. Ruan, lucid and fractured, remains a silent observer. The room is heavy with the unspoken tension that a refusal to eat has brought. The staff are still waiting, their expectations of compliance unwavering. The scent of food, once enticing, now serves as a reminder of your defiance.

## Turn 15
> stay awake

In the cell, where the day winds toward sleep, you're returned, a weary echo of your earlier self. The bed beneath you, the cup beside it, and the book on the floor, all remain unchanged from your departure. The door, devoid of a handle, remains closed, its narrow slit offering a glimpse of the corridor. The room, much like you, is cleaner but more tired.

A dull tiredness sits behind your eyes, unease under the ribs, and words come out thinner than intended. You resist sleep, fighting the urge to give in. The institution, ever watchful, expects rest.

In a contrast to your effort, hunger eases a little, as food, left as a debate for far too long, is no longer delayed. The day, though winding toward sleep, still has a flicker of life in its exhaustion.

## Turn 19
> wait

In the somber cell, the first light of a new day breaks through the slit, signaling the institution's formal collection. You, however, find yourself still within the confines of the cell, waiting. The room, softened by the weight of exhaustion, feels more like a cushion than a prison.

Despite a grand plan to stay awake, sleep overtakes you again, your face pressing against the cold wall. Upon waking, a sense of unease lingers beneath your ribs, words coming out thinner than intended. The cell remains quiet, keeping its watchful vigil as you stir.

> wait

In the dim morning, you're led from your cell, the clang of bolts echoing in the formal corridor. The institution's pace is deliberate, hands guiding you along the plain, cold walls. ## Turn 21
> wait

In the formal corridor, under the dim, unyielding light, you find yourself sandwiched between two staff members. One, a towering figure of quiet practicality, grips your arm firmly. The other, a younger, tense individual, adhering rigidly to the rules, mirrors the action. The air between you is thick with expectation, a palpable demand for compliance.

The silence is broken as the larger staff member presents a word, a name, that seems to hang in the air, a question. Unease settles under your ribs, and words come out thinner than you intend. The name they display is yours. It echoes off the cold, unyielding walls, a reminder of the institution's control. The corridor remains unchanged, the bolts on the door the same as those that confined you earlier. You wait, the institution moving at its own pace, the question hanging in the air.

--- J. NARRATOR OUTPUT ---
In the formal corridor, under the dim, unyielding light, you find yourself sandwiched between two staff members. One, a towering figure of quiet practicality, grips your arm firmly. The other, a younger, tense individual, adhering rigidly to the rules, mirrors the action. The air between you is thick with expectation, a palpable demand for compliance.

The silence is broken as the larger staff member presents a word, a name, that seems to hang in the air, a question. Unease settles under your ribs, and words come out thinner than you intend. The name they display is yours. It echoes off the cold, unyielding walls, a reminder of the institution's control. The corridor remains unchanged, the bolts on the door the same as those that confined you earlier. You wait, the institution moving at its own pace, the question hanging in the air.

## Turn 23
> wait

In the facility's sterile interview room, the air thick with unspoken tension, you wait. The room, dimly lit, houses three figures - a staff member who exudes control, another who is large and practical, and Iven, gentle and believing.

The controlled staff member taps two figures, older and younger, prompting a question that hangs in the air - "Family?" A smaller figure, possibly a brother, joins the tableau. The smell in the room triggers something within you, stirring memories that manifest without being summoned. Some feel vivid, some feel wrong.

You find yourself waiting, unease gnawing at your insides, words escaping thinner than you intend. The staff members continue their unspoken interrogation, waiting for your answer to their questions. The facility's moment continues, the stakes unchanged, the tension palpable.

In the facility's sterile interview room, the air thick with unspoken tension, you wait. The room, dimly lit, houses three figures - a staff member who exudes control, another who is large and practical, and Iven, gentle and believing.

You find yourself waiting, unease gnawing at your insides, words escaping thinner than you intend. The staff members continue their unspoken interrogation, waiting for your answer to their questions. The facility's moment continues, the stakes unchanged, the tension palpable.

> no

## Turn 29
> wait

In the dimming twilight, the stones beneath your feet remain unyielding as you stand on a jetty, the water's edge a mere arm's reach away. The facility's staff, a mix of controlled and practical individuals, continue their interrogation. A gentle believer named Iven stands among them.

The question about the other place echoes through the air, a chilling reminder of the facility's mysteries. The words hang heavy in the air, a tangible reminder of the heat, cold, foul air, noise, and unquenchable thirst that pervade that place. The fear of that place settles deep within you, a fear that seems impossible to shake off.

## Turn 30
> look around

In the facility's illuminated chamber, the table that had once been shrouded in darkness now gleams under the bright light. ## Turn 31
> examine the grate

The room, dimly lit and sterile, continues to hold you captive. Your feet, planted firmly on the cold, hard floor, do not move. The staff member, controlled and collared, continues their explanation, their words slow and deliberate. Nessa, abrasive and skeptical, listens intently.

You ask about the grate, but there is no grate here. Instead, they explain, in slow words: you continued after death. Consciousness can persist. They maintain environments for that. Heaven is reward. Hell is permanent exclusion. They do not present this as a threat. They present it as reality. Fear keeps your shoulders tight, and words come out thinner than you intend. The staff member waits, poised for your next question.

The room, dimly lit and sterile, continues to hold you captive. Your feet, planted firmly on the cold, hard floor, do not move. The staff member, controlled and collared, continues their explanation, their words slow and deliberate. Nessa, abrasive and skeptical, listens intently.

Hell is permanent exclusion. They do not present this as a threat. They present it as reality. Fear keeps your shoulders tight, and words come out thinner than you intend. The staff member waits, poised for your next question.

## Turn 32
> wait

In the sterile interview room, the air thick with tension, you find yourself waiting amidst the scrutinizing gaze of a staff member and the skeptical Nessa. The terms of a five-year research agreement are laid out before you: examinations, surgery, neurological procedures, biopsies, testing, drugs, memory experiments, sleep studies, language assessment, monitoring. The promise of five years in Heaven hangs heavy, the terms bureaucratic and unyielding. Your heart races, your palms slick, as you wait, your voice faltering as the silence stretches. The room remains still, their eyes trained on you, waiting for your decision.

In the sterile, bureaucratic interview room, the tension lingers. The staff member, clad in a collared uniform, maintains a controlled demeanor. Nessa, a skeptical figure, remains unyielding. The offer of a five-year research agreement still hangs in the air, a proposition that threatens to blur the line between service and salvation.

You find yourself in the same position, your body still, a testament to your hesitation. Words, usually fluid, seem to falter, trapped by the weight of the decision. Fear tightens your shoulders, a physical manifestation of the unease that grips you. Yet, there's no action, no words spoken. The room remains silent, the contract offer still on the table, the clock ticking away the seconds. The anticipation, the uncertainty, the fear—they all persist, a silent symphony of indecision.

The staff member's eyes remain fixed on you, a subtle tension in the air as the seconds tick by. Your heart beats faster, the rhythm echoing in your chest, and words seem to falter at the edge of your lips.
